Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the biological structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 97. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 996 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the biological space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 97 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 996. QED.
